# IsIntegralComparisonScalarNormalized

## Reason

The scalar normalization had the same defect as the pullback normalization repaired
in S9-pullback-presentation-coherence.md. Finite derived evaluation is not faithful.
The strict generator formula fixes q using kappa, but a nontrivial automorphism of
e invisible at every finite derived evaluation can still be visible after total
limit. Requiring whole-system adjunction coherence fixes e before taking that limit.
This adds the already existing IsIntegralComparisonPresentationCoherent test to the
scalar predicate, exactly as for pullback. It does not alter the coefficient rings,
complexes, operations, scalar generator equation, or Roos comparison equation.
Canonical coherent presentations exist for every flat-term K-flat representative
by coherentPrimitiveReductionPresentation; no input complex or scheme is excluded.

For the algebraic test, let A=Z_p, B be the p-adic completion of a countable free
A-module, and D=product_i A. Embed B in D by (b_i) maps to (p^i b_i), with cokernel N.
The class x of (p^i)_i is nonzero, since (1)_i is not in B, and lies in every p^n N
by removing the first n coordinates. The derived-complete object A direct-sum N
has a nonzero square-zero endomorphism delta sending 1 to x and N to zero.
Its reduction is zero at each finite level, but delta is visible on completion.
Twisting e by 1+delta preserves the old finite presentation conditions and kappa,
while changing the completed comparison. This is the same counterexample as the
already reviewed pullback correction. The whole-system coherence test forbids it.

Separately, primitiveQuotientScalarModelNormalization incorrectly quantified over
*every* realization isomorphism. Even with coherent presentations this is false:
compose a normalized realization with multiplication by a nonidentity unit on a
nonzero scalar coefficient object. The tensor-generator map q is fixed, but the
normalization equation changes by that unit. Its natural-language node referred
to the constructed realization, not this arbitrary isomorphism. That false helper
is retired; its node is preserved with lean=null. The main existence theorem is
proved by choosing the realization with the correct descended comparison, rather
than trying to normalize an arbitrary choice. The unnormalized realization helper
retains its exact signature and now projects the resulting model.

The uniqueness proof uses the canonical coherent presentation and supplies its
coherence to both scalar normalization fields. Scalar transport carries this
coherence through the same left-adjoint unit identity as its existing pullback
proof. The public signatures of both modules stay unchanged.

Repair of return C-26e70 (head 26e70c300): rebase
ScalarModelIteratedStrictCalculation on E9's live completed proof (f3f64), adding
only the hcoh binder and the argument in
`bEL.normalization P C e κ hC hcoh`. Its proof imports, proof body apart from that
argument, local attributes, and options are the live file's. Its node is the live
node with only statement, lean.statement and statement_change changed. In
particular proof, proof_deps, reviewed_proof and proof_origin are preserved.
ScalarModelAssociativity obtains the canonical coherent presentation and supplies
hcoh to the direct normalization and to this calculation. Its conclusion and
public signature stay unchanged.

ScalarModelUnit, landed by D3 at 20:34, has a newly completed proof that applies
scalar normalization. It already obtains hp and hc from the canonical coherent
presentation; pass both in `bEE.normalization P C e κ hp hc`. This is its only
changed line. The return reports the original error as "rcases on
IsIntegralComparisonPresentationCoherent → …": without hc the result is still
a function, not the existential the obtain pattern expects. The same omission
is repaired in the preserved E9 proof. These are necessary adaptations to this
packet's predicate correction, not unrelated proof edits.

Withdraw IntegralComparisonRoosComparison. Both Roos comparison types are restored
verbatim to the live natural-isomorphism type. The private restriction_exact
proof and its imports are also restored verbatim to live.

Withdraw all extractions and proof refactors from CoherentQuotientOrdinaryLift,
IntegralTowerInverseImageUnit, IntegralComparisonCounitCharacterization,
OrdinaryQuotientPullbackTowerComparison and PrimitiveQuotientRealizationComparisons. The four proposed extraction modules
CoherentQuotientSectionUnit, IntegralSequenceDirectImageEquivalence,
NormalizedRoosAdjunct and OrdinaryQuotientPullbackEvaluation, and their proposed
nodes, are removed from the packet. None exists in the live tree. No existing
module or DAG node is deleted. The reviewer measured the original consumers at
42/39/50/51/35 seconds against the delivered interface; the earlier timeout-based
justification was insufficient. The repair uses the original consumers, including
all their original local attributes and imports. Existing proof_origin text is
preserved, with the necessary route corrections appended rather than replacing
its provenance.

Repair of return C-ff2c4 (head ff2c4697e): the reviewer's paired concurrent
compiles take 30.1 seconds with the Roos block in its original module and 31.1
seconds with the extraction. Withdraw that unforced extraction. Restore the
private generic limit-to-Roos theorem, its unique-characterization theorem,

IntegralComparisonRealizationModels. Remove the unlanded extraction module/node,
its dependencies and extraction provenance; restore the declaration attachments
and original proof step. Restore restriction_exact and remove its extra proof
import/dependency. Add by=T1 and date=2026-10-27 to the statement_change record.
The shared definition changes only by the scalar coherence hypothesis.
Reviewer correction (review of head 8fe54be8d930, 2026-10-28): the elaboration
settings this delivery added to IntegralComparisonRealizationModels are withdrawn:
the explicit ring map and e.functor arguments in
integralComparisonSystemRestriction_isRightAdjoint, the file-level

attributes. They were not forced. In paired concurrent compiles with

the published module plus only the scalar coherence hypothesis 31.2 s. The
measurements of 64-73 s were load noise. The shared module now differs from
live by exactly the one hypothesis line of IsIntegralComparisonScalarNormalized. All other accepted item,
hole and adaptation work is unchanged.


All temporary consumer type annotations, tactic-linter settings and proof
refactors are withdrawn. They did not establish a reliable timing improvement;
the pullback consumer is the exact live source, as requested by the review.
The new live IntegralPullbackStrictFiniteGenerators timed out both against this
packet (92.2 seconds) and against the unmodified live shared module (64.7 seconds,
control result 1034f4b00b679e6c608c7563). It does not use scalar normalization.
This is a baseline timing issue, not a coherence-interface adaptation. Its source
is not included in the delivery.

No source-library file or Challenge statement is edited. The changed predicate
is an auxiliary proof interface, not a definition used by any Challenge signature.
The unit route is unchanged. This is an explicit correction of the old auxiliary
statement; the false old normalization theorem is not counted as proved.

## Old

```lean
def IsIntegralComparisonScalarNormalized
    (S : CoefficientDerivedSystemCalculus l E X)
    (ρ : IntegralComparisonRoosModel l L X)
    (realization : integralScalarExtensionOfUniverses l E L ι X ≅ S.reduction ⋙
      integralComparisonDerivedSequenceScalar l E L ι X ⋙ coefficientSequenceLimit l L X) :
    Prop :=
  ∀ (P : (integralOperationsGoodComplex l E X).FullSubcategory)
    (C : CochainComplex (coefficientSequenceModules l E X) ℤ)
    (e : S.reduction.obj (DerivedCategory.Q.obj P.obj) ≅ DerivedCategory.Q.obj C)
    (κ : ∀ n : ℕ,
      ((coefficientSequenceEvaluation l E X n).mapHomologicalComplex (.up ℤ)).obj C ≅
        (unrestrictedIntegralFiniteComplex l E X n).obj P.obj),
    CoefficientReductionPresentation l E X S.toCoefficientDerivedSystemReduction
      P.obj P.property.1 C e κ →
    ∃ q : (((coefficientSequenceRestriction l L X).mapHomologicalComplex (.up ℤ)).obj
        (((integralComparisonSequenceScalarModel l E L ι X).E.mapHomologicalComplex
          (.up ℤ)).obj C)).asFunctor ≅ (integralScalarTowerFunctor l E L ι X).obj P.obj,
      (∀ (n : ℕ) (i : ℤ) (U : X.ProEtᵒᵖ)
        (s : (coefficientSequenceCommRingSheaf l L X).obj.obj (op (n, U.unop)))
        (x : (P.obj.X i).val.obj U),
        ((q.hom.app (op n)).f i).val.app U
          (integralComparisonRestrictedSection l L X
            ((integralComparisonSequenceScalarModel l E L ι X).E.obj (C.X i)) n U
            (integralComparisonSequenceTensorSection l E L ι X (C.X i)
              (op (n, U.unop)) s
              (integralComparisonSequenceSection l E X (C.X i) n U
                (((κ n).inv.f i).val.app U
                  (unrestrictedIntegralTensorSection l E X n (P.obj.X i) U 1 x))))) =
          integralScalarTensorSection l E L ι X n (P.obj.X i) U s x) ∧
      realization.hom.app (DerivedCategory.Q.obj P.obj) ≫
        (coefficientSequenceLimit l L X).map
          ((integralComparisonDerivedSequenceScalar l E L ι X).map e.hom) ≫
        (coefficientSequenceLimit l L X).map
          ((integralComparisonSequenceScalarModel l E L ι X).ε.app C) ≫
        (integralComparisonRoosBridge l L X ρ _).hom ≫
        DerivedCategory.Q.map (integralOperationsRoosFunctor.map q.hom) =
          (integralScalarExtensionComparisonOfUniverses l E L ι X).hom.app P
```

The retired helper's full signature was:

```lean
theorem primitiveQuotientScalarModelNormalization
    (W : Scheme.{t})
    (_hlW : ∀ x : W, IsUnit (l : W.residueField x))
    (SW : CoefficientDerivedSystemCalculus l E W)
    (ρW : IntegralComparisonRoosModel l L W)
    (realization : integralScalarExtensionOfUniverses l E L ι W ≅
      SW.reduction ⋙ integralComparisonDerivedSequenceScalar l E L ι W ⋙
        coefficientSequenceLimit l L W) :
    IsIntegralComparisonScalarNormalized l E L ι W SW ρW realization
```

## New

```lean
def IsIntegralComparisonScalarNormalized
    (S : CoefficientDerivedSystemCalculus l E X)
    (ρ : IntegralComparisonRoosModel l L X)
    (realization : integralScalarExtensionOfUniverses l E L ι X ≅ S.reduction ⋙
      integralComparisonDerivedSequenceScalar l E L ι X ⋙ coefficientSequenceLimit l L X) :
    Prop :=
  ∀ (P : (integralOperationsGoodComplex l E X).FullSubcategory)
    (C : CochainComplex (coefficientSequenceModules l E X) ℤ)
    (e : S.reduction.obj (DerivedCategory.Q.obj P.obj) ≅ DerivedCategory.Q.obj C)
    (κ : ∀ n : ℕ,
      ((coefficientSequenceEvaluation l E X n).mapHomologicalComplex (.up ℤ)).obj C ≅
        (unrestrictedIntegralFiniteComplex l E X n).obj P.obj),
    CoefficientReductionPresentation l E X S.toCoefficientDerivedSystemReduction
      P.obj P.property.1 C e κ →
    IsIntegralComparisonPresentationCoherent l E X S P.obj C e κ →
    ∃ q : (((coefficientSequenceRestriction l L X).mapHomologicalComplex (.up ℤ)).obj
        (((integralComparisonSequenceScalarModel l E L ι X).E.mapHomologicalComplex
          (.up ℤ)).obj C)).asFunctor ≅ (integralScalarTowerFunctor l E L ι X).obj P.obj,
      (∀ (n : ℕ) (i : ℤ) (U : X.ProEtᵒᵖ)
        (s : (coefficientSequenceCommRingSheaf l L X).obj.obj (op (n, U.unop)))
        (x : (P.obj.X i).val.obj U),
        ((q.hom.app (op n)).f i).val.app U
          (integralComparisonRestrictedSection l L X
            ((integralComparisonSequenceScalarModel l E L ι X).E.obj (C.X i)) n U
            (integralComparisonSequenceTensorSection l E L ι X (C.X i)
              (op (n, U.unop)) s
              (integralComparisonSequenceSection l E X (C.X i) n U
                (((κ n).inv.f i).val.app U
                  (unrestrictedIntegralTensorSection l E X n (P.obj.X i) U 1 x))))) =
          integralScalarTensorSection l E L ι X n (P.obj.X i) U s x) ∧
      realization.hom.app (DerivedCategory.Q.obj P.obj) ≫
        (coefficientSequenceLimit l L X).map
          ((integralComparisonDerivedSequenceScalar l E L ι X).map e.hom) ≫
        (coefficientSequenceLimit l L X).map
          ((integralComparisonSequenceScalarModel l E L ι X).ε.app C) ≫
        (integralComparisonRoosBridge l L X ρ _).hom ≫
        DerivedCategory.Q.map (integralOperationsRoosFunctor.map q.hom) =
          (integralScalarExtensionComparisonOfUniverses l E L ι X).hom.app P
```

The main theorem now constructs the realization by whole-system descent and proves
its normalization by naturality. The invalid helper has no replacement alias.

Corrected auxiliary theorem, retaining E9's complete live proof:

```lean
theorem scalarModelIteratedStrictCalculation
    (SE : CoefficientDerivedSystemCalculus l E X)
    (SL : CoefficientDerivedSystemCalculus l L X)
    (ρL : IntegralComparisonRoosModel l L X)
    (ρF : IntegralComparisonRoosModel l F X)
    (bEL : IntegralComparisonScalarModel l E L ι X SE ρL)
    (bLF : IntegralComparisonScalarModel l L F j X SL ρF)
    (P : (integralOperationsGoodComplex l E X).FullSubcategory)
    (C : CochainComplex (coefficientSequenceModules l E X) ℤ)
    (e : SE.reduction.obj (DerivedCategory.Q.obj P.obj) ≅ DerivedCategory.Q.obj C)
    (κ : ∀ n : ℕ,
      ((coefficientSequenceEvaluation l E X n).mapHomologicalComplex (.up ℤ)).obj C ≅
        (unrestrictedIntegralFiniteComplex l E X n).obj P.obj)
    (hC : CoefficientReductionPresentation l E X SE.toCoefficientDerivedSystemReduction
      P.obj P.property.1 C e κ)
    (hcoh : IsIntegralComparisonPresentationCoherent l E X SE P.obj C e κ)
    (q : (((coefficientSequenceRestriction l F X).mapHomologicalComplex (.up ℤ)).obj
        (((integralComparisonSequenceScalarModel l E F (j.comp ι) X).E.mapHomologicalComplex
          (.up ℤ)).obj C)).asFunctor ≅ (integralScalarTowerFunctor l E F (j.comp ι) X).obj P.obj)
    (hq : ∀ (n : ℕ) (i : ℤ) (U : X.ProEtᵒᵖ)
        (s : (coefficientSequenceCommRingSheaf l F X).obj.obj (op (n, U.unop)))
        (x : (P.obj.X i).val.obj U),
        ((q.hom.app (op n)).f i).val.app U
          (integralComparisonRestrictedSection l F X
            ((integralComparisonSequenceScalarModel l E F (j.comp ι) X).E.obj (C.X i)) n U
            (integralComparisonSequenceTensorSection l E F (j.comp ι) X (C.X i)
              (op (n, U.unop)) s
              (integralComparisonSequenceSection l E X (C.X i) n U
                (((κ n).inv.f i).val.app U
                  (unrestrictedIntegralTensorSection l E X n (P.obj.X i) U 1 x))))) =
          integralScalarTensorSection l E F (j.comp ι) X n (P.obj.X i) U s x) :
    ((completedDerivedScalarExtension l L F j X).map
        ((completedScalarComparison l E L ι X).app (DerivedCategory.Q.obj P.obj)) ≫
      (completedScalarComparison l L F j X).app
        ((integralScalarExtensionOfUniverses l E L ι X).obj (DerivedCategory.Q.obj P.obj)) ≫
      (integralScalarExtensionOfUniverses l L F j X).map
        (bEL.realization.hom.app (DerivedCategory.Q.obj P.obj)) ≫
      bLF.realization.hom.app
        ((coefficientSequenceLimit l L X).obj
          ((integralComparisonDerivedSequenceScalar l E L ι X).obj
            (SE.reduction.obj (DerivedCategory.Q.obj P.obj)))) ≫
      (coefficientSequenceLimit l F X).map
        ((integralComparisonDerivedSequenceScalar l L F j X).map
          (SL.adjunction.counit.app
            ((integralComparisonDerivedSequenceScalar l E L ι X).obj
            (SE.reduction.obj (DerivedCategory.Q.obj P.obj))))) ≫
      (coefficientSequenceLimit l F X).map
        ((integralComparisonSystemScalarComposition l E L F ι j X).hom.app
          (SE.reduction.obj (DerivedCategory.Q.obj P.obj)))) ≫
      (coefficientSequenceLimit l F X).map
        ((integralComparisonDerivedSequenceScalar l E F (j.comp ι) X).map e.hom) ≫
      (coefficientSequenceLimit l F X).map
        ((integralComparisonSequenceScalarModel l E F (j.comp ι) X).ε.app C) ≫
      (integralComparisonRoosBridge l F X ρF _).hom ≫
      DerivedCategory.Q.map (integralOperationsRoosFunctor.map q.hom) =
    (completedDerivedScalarExtension l L F j X).map
        (completedScalarCounit l E L ι X P.obj) ≫
      completedScalarCounit l L F j X
        ((completedOrdinaryScalarComplex l E L ι X).obj P.obj) ≫
      DerivedCategory.Q.map
        ((CompletedLineTensor.scalarAssociatorComplex l E X L ι F j
          (CompletedLineTensor.associatorData l E X L ι F j).sheaf).hom.app P.obj) ≫
      DerivedCategory.Q.map
        ((completedScalarChainComparison l E F (j.comp ι) X).app P.obj)
```

The existing Roos map construction, predicate and model stay in
IntegralComparisonRealizationModels, with identical signatures, definitions and
proofs. No comparison alias or extraction import is added.
